I just... well, I really believed my heritage quilt ideas were going to put our little town on the map. It felt like I had finally found the thing that only I could make happen. I really thought my designs were what would finally get the town noticed. I didn't really stop to look at anyone else's work. I just... I've never acted like that before. I usually just keep to myself.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself feeling like you have a special destiny or that you're meant for great things?\n\nPatient: I don't... I don't think like that normally.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling unusually self-important or superior to others outside of the quilt project?\n\nPatient: No. Just the quilts. I don't...
